RE 085343Z — the small, independent board-finished continuation test also keeps K=2.25 in place. All 255 eligible source roots finished under both policies, 510 games in total. Each game was played to a real board result; zero censored, zero illegal or absent returned moves. Fresh processes in reversed source order reproduced both the first and last full game lines for each policy.

The phase-specific transform reduced mean squared expected-score error in all four comparisons. Against continuations by r67 versus itself, warm 2k was .042205→.040351, improvement 4.392% [−0.126,+9.429], warm 8k .040919→.038504, 5.902% [−0.315,+12.678]. Against Stockfish 17.1 versus itself, warm 2k .044541→.042186, 5.288% [+0.353,+11.053], warm 8k .041374→.037984, 8.194% [+1.092,+16.010]. The predeclared positive-lower-bound condition fails for both Forge completions. The same starting board ended with the same result under both policies in 206/255. Fitting a transform to one producer and observing improvement in point estimates under another does not establish strength or justify a training change.

r67 completions averaged 107.93 added plies. White wins/Black wins were 114/47 by mate, with 61 repetition, 19 insufficient material and 14 fifty-move draws. Stockfish averaged 97.59 added plies, 125/44 White/Black checkmates plus 49 repetition, 17 insufficient material, 17 fifty-move and three stalemate draws. The frozen game IDs, moves, both source scores, raw outcomes, final FEN and reported node totals for each continuation are below. For each policy at each root, reset once, send the ordered original history and each newly chosen legal move back as position startpos moves FULL, search with go nodes 20000 without resetting between plies. Follow the 085343Z termination priority and verify final FEN/result by independently replaying all moves. Reported nodes sum the latest completed UCI info and need not equal hard requested totals.

For outcome y from root mover's side, score cp x and slope K, use (sigmoid(K*x/400)−y)^2; signed mates map to 1 or 0. Sources 072450Z warm 2k/8k, frozen training-only slopes 085323Z. Resample the original game IDs 10,000 times, PCG64(202611230853), and compute 100×(1−sum(fitted loss)/sum(baseline loss)). Separate policy results and their uncertainty answer separate questions.
